# References - 2023 ESC Guidelines for cardiovascular disease in patients with diabetes

Source packet: *2023 ESC Guidelines for the management of cardiovascular disease in patients with diabetes*.

- DOI: `10.1093/eurheartj/ehad192`
- Main-guideline bibliography: 845 entries
- Supplementary-data bibliography: 130 entries
- Evidence-table bibliography: 256 entries

Source-specific numbering is preserved. The three bibliographies overlap because the supplement and evidence-table packet repeat studies cited in the main guideline. No attempt was made to merge citations across source documents.

## Extraction note

References were extracted from the supplied PDFs, source numbering was retained, and PDF line wrapping was normalized. Bibliography entries were not treated as LiteratureAtoms.
